# 99 — Coverage matrix

> Part C · Generated by `_work/scripts/coverage_check.py --write-matrix` from `_work/manifest.csv`, every coverage fragment in `_work/coverage/*.csv`, and every source reference cited in documents 00–41. Do not edit by hand — re-run the script.

## How to read this matrix

Every file in the `multiwinia/` tree (6,189 entries) has one row, grouped by area:

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **Path** | Path relative to the `multiwinia/` source root at commit `f909630`. |
| **Build** | `live` = compiled into the PC project (listed in `targets/vs2005/Darwinia.vcproj` or `contrib/eclipse/eclipse.vcproj`, not excluded in all configurations) **or** `#include`d by such a file (manifest column `included_by_live`); `excluded` = listed but excluded in every configuration; `not-in-project` = source file the project never compiles; `n/a` = data, docs, tools and other non-source files. |
| **Cited in** | Final documents (by number) that cite the file with a `path:Symbol` or path reference. 91 and 99 themselves are not counted. |
| **Depth (agents)** | Deepest read recorded in any coverage fragment (`full` / `skimmed` / `not-read`) and the Phase-2/3 agents that recorded it. |
| **Out-of-scope justification** | Why a file needs no citation (third-party library internals, uncompiled SystemIV components, dead platform code), from `_work/out_of_scope.csv` or the automatic rule for non-compiled source. |

## Acceptance checks (spec §4 Phase 4)

The script reports, and this set is complete only when each is zero or every remaining item carries a justification here:

- **(a)** live source files never cited;
- **(b)** data files never cited;
- **(c)** data formats without a grammar in [29](29_data_formats_reference.md) (registry: `_work/formats_registry.csv`);
- **(d)** classes in `code/worldobject/` without a behaviour-spec heading in [02](02_units_and_entities.md)/[03](03_buildings_and_world_objects.md)/[04](04_crates_powerups_and_weapons.md) or missing from the Mermaid class diagrams in [21](21_simulation_and_entity_model.md);
- **(e)** textures, shapes, sounds and other assets without a catalogue entry in [25](25_textures_and_assets.md) (or [10](10_audio_design.md) for audio).

The full machine-readable result is `_work/coverage_report.json`; the human-readable report is `_work/coverage_report.md`.
